Reaching for flo keeps its stack metadata (recorded parents, restack flags) consistent in ways raw `git checkout -b` / `git rebase` / `git push` would silently break. ([brunokiafuka/toolkit](https://github.com/brunokiafuka/toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/flo) · ★4)
